```python
# mph/re/index.py
"""Client MCP-stdio cho codebase-memory-mcp: index + status."""
import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from ..config import Config
from ..errors import HarnessError

_DEFAULT_CMD = None  # resolve từ Config khi None


def project_name(repo_path: Path) -> str:
    s = str(Path(repo_path).resolve())
    for ch in ":\\/ .":
        s = s.replace(ch, "-")
    while "--" in s:
        s = s.replace("--", "-")
    return s.strip("-")


class IndexClient:
    """Nói MCP stdio (JSON-RPC newline-delimited) với codebase-memory-mcp."""

    def __init__(self, cmd):
        self._cmd = list(cmd) if isinstance(cmd, (list, tuple)) else [cmd]
        self._id = 0
        self._proc = subprocess.Popen(
            self._cmd, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, text=True, encoding="utf-8")
        self._call("initialize", {
            "protocolVersion": "2024-11-05", "capabilities": {},
            "clientInfo": {"name": "mph", "version": "0.1"}})

    def _send(self, obj) -> None:
        try:
            self._proc.stdin.write(json.dumps(obj) + "\n")
            self._proc.stdin.flush()
        except (BrokenPipeError, OSError) as e:
            raise HarnessError("index server da chet (stdin)",
                                hint=str(e)) from e

    def _read(self, want_id: int, timeout_note: str = "") -> dict:
        line = self._proc.stdout.readline()
        if not line:
            raise HarnessError("index server EOF truoc response",
                                hint=f"khong nhan duoc id={want_id} {timeout_note}")
        try:
            msg = json.loads(line)
        except ValueError:
            return self._read(want_id)
        return msg

    def _call(self, method: str, params: dict | None = None) -> dict:
        self._id += 1
        body = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "method": method, "id": self._id}
        if params is not None:
            body["params"] = params
        self._send(body)
        while True:
            msg = self._read(self._id)
            if msg.get("id") == self._id:
                if "error" in msg:
                    raise HarnessError(f"index server error: {method}",
                                        hint=str(msg["error"]))
                return msg.get("result", {})

    def _tool(self, name: str, arguments: dict) -> dict:
        r = self._call("tools/call", {"name": name, "arguments": arguments})
        for part in (r.get("content") or []):
            if part.get("type") == "text":
                try:
                    return json.loads(part["text"])
                except ValueError:
                    return {"raw": part["text"]}
        return r

    def index_repo(self, repo_path: Path, mode: str = "moderate") -> dict:
        return self._tool("index_repository",
                          {"repo_path": str(Path(repo_path).resolve()),
                           "mode": mode})

    def status(self, project: str) -> dict:
        return self._tool("index_status", {"project": project})

    def close(self) -> None:
        try:
            self._proc.stdin.close()
            self._proc.wait(timeout=10)
        except OSError:
            pass


def ensure_indexed(cfg, jadx_out: Path) -> dict:
    """Index nếu chưa có; skip khi status đã ok (idempotent)."""
    cmd = getattr(cfg, "index_cmd", None) or _DEFAULT_CMD
    mode = getattr(cfg, "index_mode", "moderate")
    c = IndexClient(cmd)
    try:
        proj = project_name(jadx_out)
        st = c.status(proj)
        if st.get("status") == "ok":
            return st
        return c.index_repo(jadx_out, mode)
    finally:
        c.close()
```

def test_p3_green(tmp_path):
    from mph.selftest import run_p3
    class DepsP3(DepsFail):
        device_route_probe = staticmethod(lambda adb, port: True)
        @staticmethod
        def device_https_probe(adb, port):
            return "nocurl", "skipped"
        magisk_present = staticmethod(lambda adb: True)
        integrity_status = staticmethod(lambda adb, cfg: {"shamiko": True,
            "pif": True, "zygisk": True, "denylist": 1, "fingerprints": []})
        frida_start = staticmethod(lambda adb, cfg: "/data/local/tmp/sysmondd")
        frida_health = staticmethod(lambda adb: True)
        unpin_smoke = staticmethod(lambda adb, cfg: (0, ""))
        pull_apk = staticmethod(lambda adb, pkg, dest: dest / f"{pkg}.apk")
        decompile = staticmethod(lambda apk, out: out)
        @staticmethod
        def parse_manifest(jadx_out):
            return {"package": "x", "exported": {"activities": [".Main"]},
                    "deeplinks": [], "permissions": []}
        ensure_indexed = staticmethod(lambda cfg, out: {"status": "ok"})
    ok, rows = run_p3(FakeCfg(tmp_path), deps=DepsP3)
    names = [r[0] for r in rows]
    assert ok is True
    assert {"pull", "jadx", "manifest", "index"} <= set(names)

def test_p3_second_run_skips(tmp_path):
    # lần 2: pull/jadx/index đều skip (file có sẵn) — vẫn xanh
    from mph.selftest import run_p3
    calls = []
    class DepsP3(test_p3_green.__globals__["DepsFail"]):  # noqa
        device_route_probe = staticmethod(lambda adb, port: True)
        @staticmethod
        def device_https_probe(adb, port):
            return "nocurl", ""
        magisk_present = staticmethod(lambda adb: True)
        integrity_status = staticmethod(lambda adb, cfg: {"shamiko": True,
            "pif": True, "zygisk": True, "denylist": 1, "fingerprints": []})
        frida_start = staticmethod(lambda adb, cfg: "/x")
        frida_health = staticmethod(lambda adb: True)
        unpin_smoke = staticmethod(lambda adb, cfg: (0, ""))
        def pull_apk(self, adb, pkg, dest):  # noqa
            calls.append("pull")
            p = dest / f"{pkg}.apk"
            p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            p.write_bytes(b"x")
            return p
        @staticmethod
        def decompile(apk, out):
            calls.append("jadx")
            (out / "sources").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            return out
        @staticmethod
        def parse_manifest(jadx_out):
            (jadx_out / "resources").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            (jadx_out / "resources" / "AndroidManifest.xml").write_text(
                "<manifest/>", encoding="utf-8")
            return {"package": "x", "exported": {}, "deeplinks": [], "permissions": []}
        @staticmethod
        def ensure_indexed(cfg, out):
            calls.append("index")
            return {"status": "ok"}
    ok1, _ = run_p3(FakeCfg(tmp_path), deps=DepsP3())
    n1 = len(calls)
    ok2, rows2 = run_p3(FakeCfg(tmp_path), deps=DepsP3())
    assert ok1 and ok2
    # lần 2 vẫn gọi (không file-marker trong fake) — test thật sẽ skip; ở đây
    # chỉ chứng minh chạy 2 lần không lỗi và rows đầy đủ
    assert any(r[0] == "index" for r in rows2)
```

apks_app = typer.Typer(help="APK: list/pull/install")
re_app = typer.Typer(help="Reverse engineering: jadx/manifest/index")
app.add_typer(apks_app, name="apks"); app.add_typer(re_app, name="re")

@apks_app.command("list")
def apks_list():
    serial = avd_mod.avd_serial() or ""
    for p in apks_mod.list_packages(Adb(serial=serial) if serial else Adb()):
        print(p)

@apks_app.command("pull")
def apks_pull(package: str, app: str = typer.Option(None)):
    cfg = _load_config()
    serial = avd_mod.avd_serial(cfg.avd_name)
    ws = apks_mod.app_workspace(cfg, app or package)
    print(apks_mod.pull_apk(Adb(serial=serial), package, ws / "apk"))

@apks_app.command("install")
def apks_install(apk: Path):
    serial = avd_mod.avd_serial()
    print("installed" if apks_mod.install_apk(
        Adb(serial=serial) if serial else Adb(), Path(apk)) else "fail")

@re_app.command("jadx")
def re_jadx(app_name: str):
    cfg = _load_config()
    ws = apks_mod.app_workspace(cfg, app_name)
    apk = next((ws / "apk").glob("*.apk"), None)
    if apk is None:
        print("khong thay apk — chay: mph apks pull <pkg>")
        raise typer.Exit(code=1)
    print(jadx_mod.decompile(apk, ws / "jadx-out", cfg.jadx_bin))

@re_app.command("manifest")
def re_manifest(app_name: str):
    cfg = _load_config()
    ws = apks_mod.app_workspace(cfg, app_name)
    m = manifest_mod.parse_manifest(ws / "jadx-out")
    print(manifest_mod.write_summary(m, ws / "manifest.json"))

@re_app.command("index")
def re_index(app_name: str):
    cfg = _load_config()
    ws = apks_mod.app_workspace(cfg, app_name)
    import json as _j
    print(_j.dumps(index_mod.ensure_indexed(cfg, ws / "jadx-out"),
                   ensure_ascii=False))

selftest (`mph/selftest.py` thêm run_p3 + Deps mở rộng — target app `com.android.deskclock`):

```python
P3_APP = "com.android.deskclock"

def run_p3(cfg, deps=None):
    """P3 = P2 + pull APK + jadx + manifest + index."""
    d = deps or Deps()
    p2_ok, rows = run_p2(cfg, deps=d)
    ws = apks_mod.app_workspace(cfg, P3_APP.replace(".", "_"))
    apk_dir = ws / "apk"
    apk = apk_dir / f"{P3_APP}.apk"
    if not apk.exists():
        step_row(rows, "pull", lambda: d.pull_apk(
            d.adb_factory(d.avd_serial()), P3_APP, apk_dir))
    else:
        rows.append(("pull", True, "skipped (co san)"))
    jadx_out = ws / "jadx-out"
    if not (jadx_out / "sources").exists():
        step_row(rows, "jadx", lambda: d.decompile(apk, jadx_out))
    else:
        rows.append(("jadx", True, "skipped (co san)"))
    def _mf():
        m = d.parse_manifest(jadx_out)
        manifest_mod.write_summary(m, ws / "manifest.json")
        return m["package"] or "ok"
    step_row(rows, "manifest", _mf)
    st = {"status": "ok"}
    step_row(rows, "index", lambda: d.ensure_indexed(cfg, jadx_out))
    return p2_ok, rows
```

(trợ giúp `step_row(rows, name, fn)` dùng chung try/except như run_p1; Deps thêm 4 field mặc định nối `mph.re.*`; CLI selftest nhận `--phase p3`.)

`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-08-mobile-harness-p4-screen-mcp.md`:

```md
# Mobile Pentest Harness — P4 Screen & MCP Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Cho AI điều khiển emulator bằng mắt+ tay (screenshot → tọa độ → tap) qua **MCP server** + đo **Play Integrity verdict thật** (SPIC) — DoD `mph selftest --phase p4` xanh thêm 5 hàng.

**Architecture:** `mph/device/screen.py` (screencap binary-safe + wm-size meta + input + uiautomator), `mph/root/spic.py` (verdict), `mcp_server/server.py` (**MCP-stdio thuần stdlib**, đối xứng index client). Skills `mp-*` mỏng gọi CLI.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12 stdlib; adb; SPIC apk đã vendor (`tools/spic.apk`).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-mobile-pentest-harness-design.md` §4.1 screen, §6 MCP surface, §4.3 `mph integrity check`.

**Ruling:** fastmcp 2.14.4 **xung đột** với `mcp` pkg trong env (`streamable_http_client` ImportError) → MCP server **tự viết stdio JSON-RPC** (pattern đã chứng minh ở `mph/re/index.py`). Zero deps mới.

## Global Constraints

- Windows 11; idempotent; `HarnessError(msg, hint)`; file < 400 dòng; conventional commits; không deps mới.
- Screenshot phải **binary-safe** (PNG magic `\x89PNG`) — adb `exec-out` + bytes capture, không qua text pipe.
- Tọa độ: mọi API nhận tọa độ theo **màn hình thật** (wm size); meta kèm theo để client map scale ảnh.
- MCP server: mỗi message 1 dòng JSON; `initialize` → `notifications/initialized` → `tools/list` / `tools/call`.

## Review Focus

1. **Screencap corrupt do CRLF** (shell CR injection) — PNG magic phải được verify, sai → raise. Test: T2 `test_screenshot_rejects_corrupt`.
2. **`wm size` có Override size** (density scale) — phải lấy Override nếu có. Test: T1 `test_wm_size_prefers_override`.
3. **`input text` với khoảng trắng/ký tự đặc biệt** — escape `%s` cho space, từ chối ký tự control. Test: T1 `test_type_text_escapes`.
4. **MCP server nhận request khi đang chạy tool lâu** — đọc line loop, không chết khi 1 tool raise (trả isError). Test: T4 `test_server_survives_tool_error`.
5. **SPIC chưa từng mở (first-run dialog)** — verdict "NO_VERDICT" cấu trúc, không crash. Test: T3 `test_spic_no_verdict`.

# tests/test_screen.py
from mph.device.screen import key, swipe, tap, type_text, wm_size

class FakeAdb:
    def __init__(self, out="Physical size: 1080x2400\n"):
        self.out = out; self.calls = []
    def run(self, *args, timeout=60, device=True):
        self.calls.append(" ".join(args))
        return type("R", (), {"ok": True, "out": self.out, "err": "", "code": 0})()

def test_wm_size_basic():
    assert wm_size(FakeAdb()) == (1080, 2400)

def test_wm_size_prefers_override():
    a = FakeAdb("Physical size: 1080x2400\nOverride size: 720x1600\n")
    assert wm_size(a) == (720, 1600)

def test_tap_and_key():
    a = FakeAdb()
    tap(a, 540, 1200)
    key(a, "3")
    assert any("input tap 540 1200" in c for c in a.calls)
    assert any("input keyevent 3" in c for c in a.calls)

def test_swipe():
    a = FakeAdb()
    swipe(a, 100, 200, 300, 400, 250)
    assert any("input swipe 100 200 300 400 250" in c for c in a.calls)

def test_type_text_escapes():
    a = FakeAdb()
    type_text(a, "hello world")
    assert any("hello%sworld" in c for c in a.calls)

def test_type_text_rejects_control():
    import pytest
    from mph.errors import HarnessError
    with pytest.raises(HarnessError):
        type_text(FakeAdb(), "bad\nnewline")

```python
# adb.py thêm
def run_raw(self, *args: str, timeout: float = 60) -> tuple[int, bytes]:
    cmd = [self.adb_path] + (["-s", self.serial] if self.serial else []) + list(args)
    p = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, timeout=timeout)
    return p.returncode, p.stdout
```

```python
# mph/device/screen.py
"""Điều khiển màn hình: tọa độ theo màn hình thật (wm size)."""
from ..errors import HarnessError


def wm_size(adb) -> tuple[int, int]:
    r = adb.run("shell", "wm", "size")
    phys = over = None
    for line in (r.out or "").splitlines():
        if line.startswith("Override size:"):
            over = line.split(":", 1)[1].strip()
        elif line.startswith("Physical size:"):
            phys = line.split(":", 1)[1].strip()
    raw = over or phys
    if not raw or "x" not in raw:
        raise HarnessError("khong doc duoc wm size", hint=r.out[:100])
    w, h = raw.lower().split("x", 1)
    return int(w), int(h)


def tap(adb, x: int, y: int) -> None:
    adb.run("shell", "input", "tap", str(int(x)), str(int(y)))


def swipe(adb, x1: int, y1: int, x2: int, y2: int, ms: int = 300) -> None:
    adb.run("shell", "input", "swipe",
            str(int(x1)), str(int(y1)), str(int(x2)), str(int(y2)), str(int(ms)))


def type_text(adb, s: str) -> None:
    if any(ord(ch) < 32 or ch == '"' for ch in s):
        raise HarnessError("type_text ky tu khong ho tro",
                            hint="chi ASCII in duoc; dung key() cho dieu khien")
    adb.run("shell", "input", "text", s.replace(" ", "%s"))


def key(adb, keycode: str) -> None:
    adb.run("shell", "input", "keyevent", keycode)
```

```python
def test_screenshot_writes_png(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    import mph.device.screen as S
    png = b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n" + b"FAKEDATA" * 10
    monkeypatch.setattr(S, "_screencap_raw", lambda adb: (0, png))
    dest = tmp_path / "s.png"
    meta = S.screenshot(FakeAdb(), dest)
    assert dest.read_bytes()[:4] == b"\x89PNG"
    assert meta["path"] == str(dest) and meta["screen"] == [1080, 2400]

def test_screenshot_rejects_corrupt(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    import mph.device.screen as S
    monkeypatch.setattr(S, "_screencap_raw", lambda adb: (0, b"NOTPNG!!"))
    from mph.errors import HarnessError
    import pytest
    with pytest.raises(HarnessError):
        S.screenshot(FakeAdb(), tmp_path / "s.png")

def test_find_bounds_center(tmp_path):
    xml = tmp_path / "ui.xml"
    xml.write_text(
        '<hierarchy><node bounds="[100,200][300,400]" text="OK"/></hierarchy>',
        encoding="utf-8")
    assert S.find_bounds(xml, "OK") == (200, 300)
    assert S.find_bounds(xml, "NOPE") is None
```

- [ ] **Step 2 — FAIL** → **Step 3 — Implement (thêm vào screen.py):**

```python
import re as _re
from pathlib import Path

_PNG = b"\x89PNG"


def _screencap_raw(adb) -> tuple[int, bytes]:
    return adb.run_raw("exec-out", "screencap", "-p", timeout=30)


def screenshot(adb, dest: Path) -> dict:
    """Chụp PNG binary-safe + meta tọa độ (screen = kích thước thật)."""
    code, data = _screencap_raw(adb)
    if code != 0 or not data.startswith(_PNG):
        raise HarnessError("screencap that bai / khong phai PNG",
                            hint=f"code={code} bytes={len(data)}")
    dest = Path(dest)
    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dest.write_bytes(data)
    return {"path": str(dest), "screen": list(wm_size(adb)),
            "png_bytes": len(data)}


def ui_dump(adb, dest: Path) -> Path:
    adb.run("shell", "uiautomator", "dump", "/sdcard/ui.xml", timeout=30)
    dest = Path(dest)
    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    p = adb.run("pull", "/sdcard/ui.xml", str(dest), timeout=30)
    if not p.ok or not dest.exists():
        raise HarnessError("uiautomator dump that bai", hint=p.err)
    return dest


_BOUNDS_RE = _re.compile(r'\[(-?\d+),(-?\d+)\]\[(-?\d+),(-?\d+)\]')


def find_bounds(xml: Path, text: str) -> tuple[int, int] | None:
    """Center của node đầu có text khớp chính xác."""
    raw = Path(xml).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    for m in _re.finditer(r"<node[^>]*>", raw):
        node = m.group(0)
        if f'text="{text}"' in node:
            b = _BOUNDS_RE.search(node)
            if b:
                x1, y1, x2, y2 = map(int, b.groups())
                return (x1 + x2) // 2, (y1 + y2) // 2
    return None
```

- [ ] **Step 4 — PASS** → **Step 5 — Commit** `feat: binary-safe screenshot + ui dump/find`.

def test_parse_basic():

- [ ] **Step 2 — FAIL** → **Step 3 — Implement:**

```python
# mph/root/spic.py
"""Đo Play Integrity verdict thật bằng app SPIC + uiautomator."""
import time
from pathlib import Path

from ..device import screen
from ..errors import HarnessError

_LEVELS = ["MEETS_STRONG_INTEGRITY", "MEETS_DEVICE", "MEETS_BASIC"]
_PKG = "com.henrichs.spic"  # SPIC app package (herzhenr/spic-android)


def parse_verdict(ui_xml_text: str) -> str:
    """Trả mức CAO NHẤT tìm được trong dump; NO_VERDICT nếu không có."""
    best = None
    for lv in _LEVELS:
        if lv in ui_xml_text:
            best = lv
            break
    if best is None and "NO_INTEGRITY" in ui_xml_text:
        return "NO_INTEGRITY"
    return best or "NO_VERDICT"


def integrity_check(adb, cfg, ws: Path) -> dict:
    """Install (nếu thiếu) + mở SPIC + poll verdict; evidence screenshot."""
    have = adb.run("shell", "pm", "list", "packages")
    if _PKG not in (have.out or ""):
        apk = Path(cfg.tools_dir) / "spic.apk"
        if not apk.exists():
            raise HarnessError("thieu tools/spic.apk", hint="chay mph bootstrap")
        r = adb.run("install", "-r", str(apk), timeout=120)
        if not r.ok:
            raise HarnessError("cai SPIC that bai", hint=(r.out or "")[-200:])
    adb.run("shell", "am", "force-stop", _PKG)
    adb.run("shell", "am", "start", "-n", f"{_PKG}/MainActivity")
    ws = Path(ws)
    ws.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    verdict = "NO_VERDICT"
    dump = ws / "spic_ui.xml"
    for _ in range(6):
        time.sleep(3)
        try:
            screen.ui_dump(adb, dump)
            verdict = parse_verdict(dump.read_text(encoding="utf-8",
                                                   errors="replace"))
        except HarnessError:
            continue
        if verdict != "NO_VERDICT":
            break
    shot = ws / "spic_evidence.png"
    try:
        screen.screenshot(adb, shot)
    except HarnessError:
        shot = None
    return {"verdict": verdict,
            "evidence": str(shot) if shot else ""}
```

(SPK activity thực tế có thể khác `MainActivity` — integration step sẽ đọc `dumpsys package` để lấy launchable activity và sửa hằng số nếu cần; ghi ledger.)

# tests/test_mcp_server.py
import io, json
from mcp_server.server import TOOLS, serve

def test_tools_registry_shape():
    assert len(TOOLS) >= 10
    for name in ("mph_screenshot", "mph_tap", "mph_ui_find"):
        assert name in TOOLS

def _run_serve(messages):
    inn = io.StringIO("".join(json.dumps(m) + "\n" for m in messages))
    out = io.StringIO()
    serve(inn, out)
    return [json.loads(l) for l in out.getvalue().splitlines() if l.strip()]

def test_serve_initialize_and_tools_list():
    msgs = [
        {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": "initialize", "params": {}},
        {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 2, "method": "tools/list"},
    ]
    resp = _run_serve(msgs)
    by_id = {m.get("id"): m for m in resp}
    assert by_id[1]["result"]["protocolVersion"] == "2024-11-05"
    names = [t["name"] for t in by_id[2]["result"]["tools"]]
    assert "mph_tap" in names

def test_serve_survives_tool_error(monkeypatch):
    def boom(args): raise RuntimeError("boom")
    monkeypatch.setitem(__import__("mcp_server.server", fromlist=["TOOLS"]).TOOLS,
                        "mph_tap", boom)
    msgs = [
        {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": "initialize", "params": {}},
        {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 2, "method": "tools/call",
         "params": {"name": "mph_tap", "arguments": {"x": 1, "y": 2}}},
        {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 3, "method": "tools/list"},
    ]
    resp = _run_serve(msgs)
    by_id = {m.get("id"): m for m in resp}
    assert by_id[2]["result"]["isError"] is True
    assert by_id[3]  # server còn sống

- [ ] **Step 2 — FAIL** → **Step 3 — Implement:**

```python
# mcp_server/server.py
"""MCP-stdio server thuần stdlib cho mph (đối xứng mph/re/index client).

Chạy: python -m mcp_server.server  (stdio, newline-delimited JSON)
Đăng ký vào Claude: .mcp.json {"mcpServers": {"mph": {
    "command": "python", "args": ["-m", "mcp_server.server"],
    "cwd": "<repo>"}}}
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))

from mph.config import load_config                    # noqa: E402
from mph.device import screen as screen_mod           # noqa: E402
from mph.device import avd as avd_mod                 # noqa: E402
from mph.device.adb import Adb                        # noqa: E402
from mph.doctor import check_env, Probes              # noqa: E402
from mph.re import apks as apks_mod                   # noqa: E402
from mph.root import spic as spic_mod                 # noqa: E402
from mph.selftest import run_p1                       # noqa: E402


def _adb():
    cfg = load_config()
    serial = avd_mod.avd_serial(cfg.avd_name)
    return Adb(serial=serial) if serial else Adb()


def _ws():
    return Path(load_config().workspace) / "_shared"


def t_doctor(args):
    rows = check_env(load_config(), Probes(devices=lambda: avd_mod.Adb and []))
    return {"rows": [[n, ok, h] for n, ok, h in rows]}


def t_screenshot(args):
    dest = _ws() / "shots" / "screen.png"
    import time
    dest = _ws() / "shots" / f"screen-{time.strftime('%H%M%S')}.png"
    return screen_mod.screenshot(_adb(), dest)


def t_tap(args):
    screen_mod.tap(_adb(), args["x"], args["y"])
    return {"ok": True}


def t_swipe(args):
    screen_mod.swipe(_adb(), args["x1"], args["y1"], args["x2"],
                     args["y2"], args.get("ms", 300))
    return {"ok": True}


def t_type(args):
    screen_mod.type_text(_adb(), args["text"])
    return {"ok": True}


def t_key(args):
    screen_mod.key(_adb(), args["keycode"])
    return {"ok": True}


def t_ui_find(args):
    adb = _adb()
    dump = _ws() / "ui.xml"
    screen_mod.ui_dump(adb, dump)
    center = screen_mod.find_bounds(dump, args["text"])
    return {"center": center, "dump": str(dump)}


def t_packages(args):
    return {"packages": apks_mod.list_packages(_adb())}


def t_integrity_check(args):
    cfg = load_config()
    return spic_mod.integrity_check(_adb(), cfg, _ws() / "spic")


def t_selftest_phase(args):
    ok, rows = run_p1(load_config())
    return {"ok": ok, "rows": [[n, o, d[:120]] for n, o, d in rows]}


TOOLS = {
    "mph_doctor": t_doctor,
    "mph_screenshot": t_screenshot,
    "mph_tap": t_tap,
    "mph_swipe": t_swipe,
    "mph_type": t_type,
    "mph_key": t_key,
    "mph_ui_find": t_ui_find,
    "mph_packages": t_packages,
    "mph_integrity_check": t_integrity_check,
    "mph_selftest_phase": t_selftest_phase,
}

_SCHEMAS = {  # mô tả tối giản cho tools/list
    "mph_screenshot": {"type": "object", "properties": {}},
    "mph_tap": {"type": "object", "properties": {
        "x": {"type": "integer"}, "y": {"type": "integer"}},
        "required": ["x", "y"]},
    "mph_swipe": {"type": "object", "properties": {
        "x1": {"type": "integer"}, "y1": {"type": "integer"},
        "x2": {"type": "integer"}, "y2": {"type": "integer"},
        "ms": {"type": "integer"}}},
    "mph_type": {"type": "object", "properties": {
        "text": {"type": "string"}}, "required": ["text"]},
    "mph_key": {"type": "object", "properties": {
        "keycode": {"type": "string"}}, "required": ["keycode"]},
    "mph_ui_find": {"type": "object", "properties": {
        "text": {"type": "string"}}, "required": ["text"]},
}


def serve(stdin, stdout) -> None:
    """Loop JSON-lines; tool raise → isError, server sống tiếp."""
    for line in stdin:
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        try:
            msg = json.loads(line)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        mid = msg.get("id")
        method = msg.get("method")
        if method == "initialize":
            _reply(stdout, {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": mid, "result": {
                "protocolVersion": "2024-11-05",
                "capabilities": {"tools": {}},
                "serverInfo": {"name": "mph", "version": "0.1"}}})
        elif method == "notifications/initialized":
            continue
        elif method == "tools/list":
            tools = [{"name": n, "description": n,
                      "inputSchema": _SCHEMAS.get(n, {"type": "object"})}
                     for n in TOOLS]
            _reply(stdout, {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": mid,
                            "result": {"tools": tools}})
        elif method == "tools/call":
            name = (msg.get("params") or {}).get("name")
            args = (msg.get("params") or {}).get("arguments") or {}
            fn = TOOLS.get(name)
            if fn is None:
                _reply(stdout, {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": mid, "result": {
                    "isError": True,
                    "content": [{"type": "text", "text": f"no tool {name}"}]}})
                continue
            try:
                result = fn(args)
                _reply(stdout, {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": mid, "result": {
                    "content": [{"type": "text",
                                 "text": json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False,
                                                    default=str)}]}})
            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — isError cho client
                _reply(stdout, {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": mid, "result": {
                    "isError": True,
                    "content": [{"type": "text", "text": str(e)[:500]}]}})


def _reply(stdout, obj) -> None:
    stdout.write(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    stdout.flush()


def main() -> None:
    serve(sys.stdin, sys.stdout)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

- [ ] **Step 4 — PASS** → **Step 5 — Commit** `feat: stdlib mcp-stdio server (10 tools)`.

`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-mobile-harness-p5-audit-engine.md`:

```md
# Mobile Pentest Harness — P5 Audit Quality Engine Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Bộ máy audit đo được không cần AI: findings model (candidate → verified/rejected), static sweep pass-2 trên jadx-out, coverage manifest MASTG, render REPORT.md, và benchmark `mph bench` tính recall/precision trên app có ground-truth.

**Architecture:** `mph/audit/` gồm 5 module thuần stdlib (findings/sweep/coverage/report/bench). Sweep quét `.java/.kt` trong `jadx-out/sources` bằng regex rules + tái dùng `parse_manifest` (P3) cho rules manifest-side. AI (P6) chỉ là một nguồn candidates khác ghi vào cùng `findings.json` — schema chung 2 chế độ manual/ai.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12 stdlib (re/json/dataclasses/pathlib). Không deps mới.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-mobile-pentest-harness-design.md` §7 (7.1–7.4), §6c, roadmap P5.

**Definition of done:** `mph audit sweep/report/verify` + `mph bench` chạy được end-to-end trên 1 app thật đã jadx; bench in recall/precision; suite test xanh toàn bộ.

## Global Constraints

- Windows 11; Python 3.12 stdlib only; file < 400 dòng; `HarnessError(msg, hint)` cho mọi lỗi người dùng thấy.
- Mọi file đọc từ APK (java/xml render bởi jadx) là **untrusted**: đọc `errors="replace"`, không bao giờ eval/exec nội dung.
- Đường dẫn trong finding là **relpath so với jadx-out** (portable, không lộ abs path máy).
- Không claim gì chưa làm: mọi finding mặc định `candidate`; chỉ `verified` mới vào phần chính của REPORT (spec §7.2/§7.3).
- Conventional commits; tiếng Việt không dấu trong code identifier/comment, report hiển thị tiếng Việt có dấu OK (UTF-8).

## Review Focus

1. **FP flood từ third-party code** (androidx/com.google/gson… trong sources) — sweep phải skip package ngoài app, giới hạn mỗi rule 50 hit; test T2 `test_sweep_skips_third_party_and_caps`.
2. **File binary/lớn đốt RAM** — file >2MB hoặc chứa NUL byte → skip; test T2 `test_sweep_skips_large_and_binary`.
3. **findings.json hỏng/dở dang** — load phải tolerant (dựng rỗng + backup file hỏng); test T1 `test_load_corrupt_json_falls_back_empty`.
4. **CRLF/BOM trong java render** — line number và evidence phải đúng (strip `\r`, bỏ BOM); test T2 `test_sweep_crlf_line_numbers`.
5. **Ground-truth `file_hint` sai casing/đường dẫn con** — match vẫn `contains` case-insensitive; test T5 `test_match_file_hint_fuzzy`.
6. **Severity lạ/nghịch**
   — normalize về lower trước khi soạn отчет; test T4 `test_report_sorts_and_normalizes_severity`.

### Task 1: Findings model + persist

**Files:**
- Create: `mph/audit/__init__.py` (rỗng)
- Create: `mph/audit/findings.py`
- Test: `tests/test_findings.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `Finding` dataclass (`id, rule, title, severity, mstg, file, line, evidence, note, reject_reason`), `.key() -> tuple`; `Findings` class (`candidates/verified/rejected: list[Finding]`, `add(f)->bool`, `verify(fid)`, `reject(fid, reason)`, `save(path)`, `Findings.load(path)`); hằng `SEVERITY_ORDER`.

- [ ] **Step 1 — failing tests:**

```python
# tests/test_findings.py
import json
from mph.audit.findings import Finding, Findings, SEVERITY_ORDER


def _f(rule="SEC-01", file="a.java", line=3, sev="high", **kw):
    return Finding(id="", rule=rule, title="t", severity=sev, mstg="M",
                   file=file, line=line, **kw)


def test_add_assigns_sequential_ids_and_dedupes():
    fs = Findings()
    assert fs.add(_f()) is True
    assert fs.add(_f()) is False          # trùng key (rule,file,line)
    assert fs.add(_f(line=4)) is True
    assert [c.id for c in fs.candidates] == ["F-001", "F-002"]


def test_verify_moves_candidate():
    fs = Findings()
    fs.add(_f())
    fs.verify("F-001")
    assert not fs.candidates and fs.verified[0].id == "F-001"


def test_reject_keeps_reason():
    fs = Findings()
    fs.add(_f())
    fs.reject("F-001", "khong reproduces")
    assert fs.rejected[0].reject_reason == "khong reproduces"


def test_save_load_roundtrip(tmp_path):
    fs = Findings()
    fs.add(_f()); fs.add(_f(line=9, rule="WEBVIEW-01", sev="medium"))
    fs.verify("F-002"); fs.reject("F-001", "FP")
    p = tmp_path / "findings.json"
    fs.save(p)
    fs2 = Findings.load(p)
    assert len(fs2.verified) == 1 and fs2.verified[0].rule == "WEBVIEW-01"
    assert fs2.rejected[0].reject_reason == "FP"
    # add sau load tiếp tục id không đụng
    assert fs2.add(_f(line=99)) and fs2.candidates[-1].id == "F-003"


def test_load_corrupt_json_falls_back_empty(tmp_path):
    p = tmp_path / "findings.json"
    p.write_text("{ halves", encoding="utf-8")
    fs = Findings.load(p)
    assert fs.candidates == [] and fs.verified == []
    assert (tmp_path / "findings.json.corrupt").exists()  # backup file hỏng


def test_load_missing_file(tmp_path):
    fs = Findings.load(tmp_path / "nope.json")
    assert fs.candidates == []
```

- [ ] **Step 2 — Run:** `python -m pytest tests/test_findings.py -q` → FAIL (ModuleNotFoundError).
- [ ] **Step 3 — implementation:** viết `mph/audit/findings.py` với `Finding` dataclass, `_norm_sev` (lower + fallback "info"), `Findings._next_id()` quét max `F-###` trên cả 3 list; `save` ghi `{"candidates": [...], "verified": [...], "rejected": [...]}` qua `asdict`; `load` đọc json, lỗi ValueError/OSError → backup `.corrupt` + trả `Findings()` rỗng.
- [ ] **Step 4 — Run:** PASS toàn file.
- [ ] **Step 5 — Commit:** `feat: P5 T1 findings model + findings.json persist`.

### Task 2: Static sweep (pass-2 engine) trên jadx-out

**Files:**
- Create: `mph/audit/sweep.py`
- Test: `tests/test_sweep.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Finding`, `Findings` (T1); `mph.re.manifest.parse_manifest` (P3).
- Produces: `RULES: list[dict]` (mỗi rule: `id, title, severity, mstg, pattern: re.Pattern, cap: int`); `SWEEP_SKIP_PREFIXES = ("androidx/", "com/google/", "kotlin/", "kotlinx/", "org/slf4j/", "okhttp3/", "okio/", "retrofit2/", "io/reactivex/", "de/robv/")`; `sweep_dir(jadx_out: Path, package_hint: str = "") -> list[Finding]` (file relpath, id rỗng — caller gán qua `Findings.add`); `manifest_findings(manifest: dict) -> list[Finding]`.

- [ ] **Step 1 — failing tests:**

```python
# tests/test_sweep.py
from pathlib import Path
from mph.audit.sweep import sweep_dir, manifest_findings, RULES


def _mk(root, rel, content):
    p = Path(root) / rel
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    p.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    return p


def test_rule_ids_unique():
    ids = [r["id"] for r in RULES]
    assert len(ids) == len(set(ids))


def test_sweep_finds_secret_and_line_numbers(tmp_path):
    _mk(tmp_path, "sources/com/vuln/Keys.java",
        'package com.vuln;\npublic class Keys {\n'
        '    String API_KEY = "AKIA1234567890ABCDE";\n}\n')
    hits = sweep_dir(tmp_path)
    by_rule = {f.rule: f for f in hits}
    assert "SEC-01" in by_rule
    f = by_rule["SEC-01"]
    assert f.file.replace("\\", "/") == "sources/com/vuln/Keys.java"
    assert f.line == 3 and "AKIA" in f.evidence


def test_sweep_skips_third_party_and_caps(tmp_path):
    _mk(tmp_path, "sources/androidx/Helper.java",
        'String password = "supersecretvalue123";\n' * 80)
    _mk(tmp_path, "sources/com/app/One.java",
        'String password = "supersecretvalue123";\n')
    hits = [f for f in sweep_dir(tmp_path) if f.rule == "SEC-01"]
    assert len(hits) == 1 and "com/app" in hits[0].file.replace("\\", "/")


def test_sweep_skips_large_and_binary(tmp_path):
    _mk(tmp_path, "sources/com/app/Big.java", "x" * (3 * 1024 * 1024))
    _mk(tmp_path, "sources/com/app/Bin.java", "a\x00b\nString password = \"supersecretvalue123\";\n")
    hits = [f for f in sweep_dir(tmp_path) if "com/app" in f.file]
    assert hits == []


def test_sweep_crlf_line_numbers(tmp_path):
    p = Path(tmp_path) / "sources/com/app/Crlf.java"
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    p.write_bytes(b"package com.app;\r\nString password = \"supersecretvalue123\";\r\n")
    hits = [f for f in sweep_dir(tmp_path) if f.rule == "SEC-01"]
    assert hits and hits[0].line == 2


def test_manifest_findings(tmp_path):
    m = {"package": "com.v", "debuggable": True, "allowBackup": True,
         "exported": {"activities": ["com.v.Main"], "services": [],
                       "receivers": ["com.v.R1"], "providers": [], "aliases": []},
         "deeplinks": [{"component": "com.v.Main", "schemes": ["myapp"],
                        "hosts": [], "mimeTypes": []}]}
    rules = {f.rule: f for f in manifest_findings(m)}
    assert set(rules) >= {"IPC-01", "DEEPLINK-01", "CONF-01", "CONF-02"}
    assert "com.v.Main" in rules["IPC-01"].evidence
```

- [ ] **Step 2 — Run:** FAIL (ModuleNotFoundError).
- [ ] **Step 3 — implementation:** `RULES` gồm ít nhất: `SEC-01` secret hardcode — `(?i)(api[_-]?key|secret|token|password)\s*=\s*"[A-Za-z0-9+/_=-]{16,}"`; `CRYPTO-01` — `MessageDigest\.getInstance\(\s*"(MD5|SHA-1|SHA1)"`; `CRYPTO-02` — `Cipher\.getInstance\(\s*"[^"]*ECB`; `WEBVIEW-01` — `setJavaScriptEnabled\(\s*true`; `WEBVIEW-02` — `addJavascriptInterface\(`; `WEBVIEW-03` — `loadUrl\(\s*"file://`; `STORAGE-01` — `MODE_WORLD_(READABLE|WRITABLE)`; `LOG-01` — `Log\.[vdi]\([^)]*(?i)(password|token|secret)`; `CERT-01` — `implements\s+X509TrustManager`; `CERT-02` — `HostnameVerifier` cùng dòng có `return true`; `ROOT-01` — `"/(system|xbin|sbin)/su"|"/dev/.magisk"`; `CLEARTEXT-01` — `"http://[^\"]+"` (bỏ http://schemas.android/). `sweep_dir`: `rglob("*.java") + rglob("*.kt")`, relpath posix, skip nếu phần tử đầu sau `sources/` thuộc `SWEEP_SKIP_PREFIXES` HOẶC `package_hint` given và không khớp, size >2MB, hoặc `\x00` trong 4KB đầu; đọc `errors="replace"`, splitlines, strip BOM dòng đầu; mỗi rule giới hạn `cap=50` hit/toàn sweep. `manifest_findings`: IPC-01 (nếu có exported), DEEPLINK-01, CONF-01 allowBackup=True, CONF-02 debuggable=True, file="AndroidManifest.xml", evidence join names.
- [ ] **Step 4 — Run:** PASS.
- [ ] **Step 5 — Commit:** `feat: P5 T2 static sweep engine + manifest rules`.

### Task 3: Coverage manifest MASTG

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `MASTG_ITEMS: list[tuple[str, str]]` (id + tên ngắn); `Coverage` class: `mark(item_id, via, note="")`, `gap(item_id, reason)`, `rows() -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]` (item, trạng thái, chi tiết), `render() -> list[str]` (dòng markdown bảng); `coverage_after_sweep(manifest_ok: bool) -> Coverage` (đánh dấu sẵn các mục pass 1–2 phủ được, gap phần dynamic).

- [ ] **Step 1 — failing tests:**

```python
# tests/test_coverage.py
from mph.audit.coverage import Coverage, MASTG_ITEMS, coverage_after_sweep


def test_items_present():
    ids = [i for i, _ in MASTG_ITEMS]
    assert len(ids) >= 10 and len(set(ids)) == len(ids)


def test_mark_gap_render():
    c = Coverage()
    c.mark(MASTG_ITEMS[0][0], "pass 2", "sweep")
    c.gap(MASTG_ITEMS[1][0], "thieu creds")
    lines = c.render()
    assert any(MASTG_ITEMS[0][0] in l and "pass 2" in l for l in lines)
    assert any(MASTG_ITEMS[1][0] in l and "thieu creds" in l for l in lines)


def test_unmarked_shows_pending():
    c = Coverage()
    c.mark(MASTG_ITEMS[0][0], "pass 1")
    assert any("chưa kiểm" in l or "chua kiem" in l
               for l in c.render())


def test_after_sweep_marks_static_gaps_dynamic():
    c = coverage_after_sweep(manifest_ok=True)
    assert any("pass 2" in r[1] for r in c.rows())
    assert any(r[1].startswith("gap") for r in c.rows())  # dynamic còn gap
```

- [ ] **Step 2 — Run:** FAIL.
- [ ] **Step 3 — implementation:** `MASTG_ITEMS` ≈ 12 dòng rút gọn từ MASTG v2 (storage, crypto, auth, network/ATS, platform IPC, webview, code quality/repackaging, anti-tamper, anti-debug, user privacy, payment, supply chain) — id dạng `MASTG-*-N`. `Coverage` giữ `dict[str, tuple[str, str]]`; `render()` in bảng markdown `| mục | trạng thái | chi tiết |`; mục chưa mark → trạng thái "chưa kiểm".
- [ ] **Step 4 — Run:** PASS.
- [ ] **Step 5 — Commit:** `feat: P5 T3 coverage manifest MASTG`.

```python
# tests/test_report.py
from pathlib import Path
from mph.audit.coverage import Coverage
from mph.audit.findings import Finding, Findings
from mph.audit.report import render_report, write_report


def _fs():
    fs = Findings()
    fs.add(Finding(id="", rule="SEC-01", title="Secret hardcode",
                   severity="HIGH", mstg="MASTG-STORAGE-1",
                   file="sources/a.java", line=3, evidence='API_KEY = "x"'))
    fs.add(Finding(id="", rule="WEBVIEW-01", title="JS enabled",
                   severity="medium", mstg="MASTG-PLATFORM-5",
                   file="sources/b.java", line=9, evidence="setJavaScriptEnabled(true)"))
    fs.verify("F-002")
    fs.reject("F-001", "FP")
    return fs


def test_report_sorts_and_normalizes_severity():
    md = render_report("com_foo", "com.foo", _fs(), Coverage())
    assert md.index("medium") < md.index("HIGH") or "medium" in md  # có đủ
    assert "high" in md  # HIGH đã normalize lower khi render tiêu đề
    assert "chưa được xác minh" in md or "chua duoc xac minh" in md  # candidates section


def test_report_has_reject_reason_and_coverage():
    md = render_report("com_foo", "com.foo", _fs(), Coverage())
    assert "FP" in md and "MASTG" in md


def test_write_report_path(tmp_path):

    assert p == tmp_path / "report" / "REPORT.md" and p.exists()
```

### Task 5: Benchmark `mph bench` + ground-truth

**Files:**
- Create: `mph/audit/bench.py`
- Create: `bench/groundtruth/ovaa.json` (ground-truth thật cho OVAA — mục static sweep phủ được)
- Test: `tests/test_bench.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `sweep_dir`, `manifest_findings` (T2).
- Produces: `load_groundtruth(path) -> list[dict]` (`{id, rule, title, file_hint?}`); `match(findings, gt) -> tuple[list, list]` (gt matched, findings unmatched); `metrics(matched_n, gt_n, found_n) -> dict` (`recall`, `precision`); `bench_run(name, jadx_out: Path, gt_path: Path, out_md: Path) -> dict` (chạy sweep + match + metrics, append bảng vào `out_md`).

- [ ] **Step 1 — failing tests:**

```python
# tests/test_bench.py
import json
from pathlib import Path
from mph.audit.bench import load_groundtruth, match, metrics, bench_run
from mph.audit.findings import Finding


def _gt(tmp_path, items):
    p = tmp_path / "gt.json"
    p.write_text(json.dumps(items), encoding="utf-8")
    return p


def _f(rule, file):
    return Finding(id="", rule=rule, title="t", severity="high", mstg="M",
                   file=file, line=1)


def test_match_rule_and_fuzzy_file_hint():
    gt = [{"id": "V1", "rule": "SEC-01", "file_hint": "keys.java"}]
    got = [_f("SEC-01", "Sources/com/ovaa/Keys.JAVA")]
    m, extra = match(got, load_groundtruth(_gt(Path("."), gt)))
    assert len(m) == 1 and not extra


def test_match_rule_only_when_no_hint():
    gt = [{"id": "V1", "rule": "IPC-01"}]
    m, extra = match([_f("IPC-01", "AndroidManifest.xml")],
                     load_groundtruth(_gt(Path("."), gt)))
    assert len(m) == 1


def test_metrics_math():
    m = metrics(matched=7, gt=10, found=8)
    assert abs(m["recall"] - 0.7) < 1e-9
    assert abs(m["precision"] - 7 / 8) < 1e-9


def test_bench_run_appends_md(tmp_path):
    src = tmp_path / "jadx" / "sources" / "com" / "ovaa"
    src.mkdir(parents=True)
    (src / "Keys.java").write_text(
        'String api_key = "AKIA1234567890ABCDE";\n', encoding="utf-8")
    gt = _gt(tmp_path, [{"id": "V1", "rule": "SEC-01",
                         "file_hint": "keys.java"}])
    out = tmp_path / "results.md"
    r = bench_run("ovaa", tmp_path / "jadx", gt, out)
    assert r["recall"] == 1.0 and r["precision"] == 1.0
    text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "ovaa" in text and "recall" in text
```

- [ ] **Step 2 — Run:** FAIL.
- [ ] **Step 3 — implementation:** `match`: gt khớp nếu `f.rule == gt["rule"]` và (không `file_hint` hoặc `file_hint.lower() in f.file.lower()`); mỗi finding chỉ khớp 1 gt (danh sách matched đánh dấu). `bench_run`: gọi `sweep_dir` + đọc manifest nếu có → findings thô; matched → `metrics(len(matched), len(gt), len(findings))`; append dòng `| name | ngày | recall | precision | n_findings |` vào `out_md` (tạo header nếu file mới).
- [ ] **Step 4 — Run:** PASS.
- [ ] **Step 5 — Commit:** `feat: P5 T5 benchmark engine + ground-truth schema`.

# Mobile Pentest Harness — P6 Runner Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Một lệnh duy nhất `run.ps1 <apk>` từ đầu tới `REPORT.md` — chế độ AI (opencode + deepseek-v4.1-flash mặc định, claude thay thế) và chế độ manual không đụng AI; resume được khi chạy lại.

**Architecture:** `run.ps1` wrapper mỏng → `python -m mph run <apk>`. Runner orchestration trong `mph/runner/` (state machine 4 phase: setup → sweep → ai → report) tái dùng `setup_manual` (P4) cho preflight idempotent, `mph.audit` (P5) cho sweep nền + report render. Agent engine trừu tượng hóa trong `mph/runner/agent.py`: render prompt từ template → chạy engine (opencode/claude) full-permission, mọi stdout ghi `run.log`, deadline + kill PID.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12 stdlib; opencode CLI (npm) hoặc claude CLI — engine hoán đổi qua `mph.toml [agent]`.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-mobile-pentest-harness-design.md` §6b (autonomous runner), §6c (chế độ manual), §11 risks (runaway/kill-switch).

**Definition of done:** `.\run.ps1 app.apk --mode manual` chạy thật từ đầu tới khi in next-steps playbook; `--mode ai` (engine cấu hình) chạy preflight → agent → REPORT.md, resume khi chạy lại; thiếu opencode/API key thì in hint cài đặt và exit mã rõ ràng. Suite xanh toàn bộ.

## Global Constraints

- Windows 11; stdlib only; file < 400 dòng; `HarnessError(msg, hint)`.
- Guardrails hành vi nằm trong **prompt + AGENTS.md** (spec §6b): chỉ workspace/<app>/, cấm tấn công chủ động remote, dừng khi hết budget — permission engine là allow-all nhưng rails là prompt.
- **Kill switch bắt buộc**: mọi engine chạy qua Popen với deadline; quá hạn → kill PID con; run.log luôn được ghi (kể cả khi agent crash).
- Prompt render bằng **token replace** (`{{PACKAGE}}`, `{{WORKSPACE}}`, `{{APK}}`), không dùng `str.format` (JSON brace trong opencode.json.tpl sẽ vỡ format).
- `--mode manual` tuyệt đối không gọi engine nào, không đọc API key (spec §6c).
- AI phase fail → vẫn chạy phase report trên dữ liệu sweep sẵn có (fail-soft), exit code khác 0.

## Review Focus

1. **opencode chưa cài / DEEPSEEK_API_KEY thiếu** — preflight phải bắt TRƯỚC khi setup 10 phút chạy oan; in hint `npm i -g opencode-ai` / set key; test T3 `test_preflight_catches_missing_engine`.
2. **Agent treo không thoát** — deadline hết → process bị kill, run.log còn nguyên, exit code 124; test T3 `test_run_engine_deadline_kills`.
3. **Chạy lại làm lại từ đầu** — state.json phase done phải skip (resume); test T2 `test_resume_skips_done`.
4. **Template chứa `{}` JSON** — render phải thay token không qua format; test T4 `test_render_prompt_no_format_braces`.
5. **mode manual chạm AI** — không import/khởi engine, không env key; test T5 `test_manual_mode_never_calls_engine`.
6. **AI dở vẫn có report** — exit khác 0 của engine không chặn report phase; test T5 `test_ai_fail_still_reports`.

# tests/test_config.py (append)
def test_agent_defaults_and_override(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("BURP_PATH", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    tmp_path.joinpath("mph.toml").write_text(
        "[agent]\nengine = \"claude\"\nmodel = \"x\"\ndeadline_min = 5\n",
        encoding="utf-8")
    cfg = load_config(tmp_path / "mph.toml")

- [ ] **Step 1 — failing tests:**

```python
# tests/test_runner_state.py
from mph.runner.state import PHASES, RunState


def test_phases_order():
    assert PHASES == ("setup", "sweep", "ai", "report")


def test_mark_and_pending(tmp_path):
    st = RunState(tmp_path / "state.json")
    st.mark_done("setup")
    assert st.pending() == ["sweep", "ai", "report"]
    assert (tmp_path / "state.json").exists()


def test_resume_skips_done(tmp_path):
    p = tmp_path / "state.json"
    RunState(p).mark_done("setup")
    st2 = RunState.load(p)
    assert "setup" in st2.done and st2.pending()[0] == "sweep"


def test_load_corrupt(tmp_path):
    p = tmp_path / "state.json"
    p.write_text("{", encoding="utf-8")
    st = RunState.load(p)
    assert st.done == set()
```

- [ ] **Step 2 — Run:** FAIL.
- [ ] **Step 3 — implementation:** json dump/load, KeyError/ValueError/OSError → rỗng.
- [ ] **Step 4 — Run:** PASS.
- [ ] **Step 5 — Commit:** `feat: P6 T2 run state machine + resume`.

### Task 3: Agent engine (render + preflight + run có kill switch)

**Files:**
- Create: `mph/runner/agent.py`
- Test: `tests/test_runner_agent.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Config` (T1).
- Produces:
  - `render_prompt(tpl_path: Path, package: str, workspace_app: str, apk: str) -> str` — thay `{{PACKAGE}}/{{WORKSPACE_APP}}/{{APK}}`.
  - `render_opencode_json(tpl_path: Path, repo_root: Path, model: str) -> str` — thay `{{REPO_ROOT}}/{{MODEL}}`.
  - `preflight(cfg) -> list[tuple[str, bool, str]]` — rows: `engine-binary` (shutil.which), `api-key` (`DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` khi engine opencode), `config-template` (tpl tồn tại).
  - `build_cmd(cfg, prompt_path: Path) -> list[str]` — opencode: `["opencode", "run", "--model", cfg.agent_model, "-"]` (prompt qua stdin) ; claude: `["claude", "-p", "--dangerously-skip-permissions", "--max-turns", str(cfg.agent_max_turns), "-"]`.
  - `run_engine(cmd, cwd, log_path, deadline_min, stdin_text, popen=subprocess.Popen, clock=time.monotonic, sleep=time.sleep) -> int` — spawn (stdin=PIPE ghi prompt), đọc stdout từng dòng append `run.log` + in, theo dõi deadline → kill; trả returncode (124 khi bị kill).

- [ ] **Step 1 — failing tests:**

```python
# tests/test_runner_agent.py
import json
from pathlib import Path
import pytest
from mph.runner import agent


def _cfg(engine="opencode"):
    class C:
        agent_engine = engine
        agent_model = "deepseek/deepseek-v4.1-flash"
        agent_max_turns = 10
        agent_deadline_min = 1
    return C()


def test_preflight_catches_missing_engine(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(agent.shutil, "which", lambda n: None)
    monkeypatch.delenv("DEEPSEEK_API_KEY", raising=False)
    rows = agent.preflight(_cfg())
    names = {r[0]: r for r in rows}
    assert names["engine-binary"][1] is False and "opencode" in names["engine-binary"][2]
    assert names["api-key"][1] is False


def test_render_prompt_no_format_braces(tmp_path):
    tpl = tmp_path / "p.md"
    tpl.write_text("App {{PACKAGE}} tại {{WORKSPACE_APP}}\njson: {\"a\": 1}\n",
                   encoding="utf-8")
    out = agent.render_prompt(tpl, "com.foo", "com_foo", "C:/a.apk")
    assert "com.foo" in out and "com_foo" in out and '{"a": 1}' in out


def test_render_opencode_json(tmp_path):
    tpl = tmp_path / "oc.json.tpl"
    tpl.write_text('{"model": "{{MODEL}}", "cwd": "{{REPO_ROOT}}"}',
                   encoding="utf-8")
    out = agent.render_opencode_json(tpl, Path("C:/r"), "m1")
    d = json.loads(out)
    assert d["model"] == "m1" and d["cwd"].replace("\\", "/") == "C:/r"


def test_build_cmd_variants():
    assert agent.build_cmd(_cfg(), Path("p.md"))[0] == "opencode"
    assert agent.build_cmd(_cfg("claude"), Path("p.md"))[0] == "claude"
    assert "--dangerously-skip-permissions" in agent.build_cmd(_cfg("claude"), Path("p.md"))


def test_run_engine_deadline_kills(tmp_path):
    class FakeProc:
        pid = 4242
        returncode = None
        def __init__(self, *a, **k): pass
        def kill(self):
            self.returncode = 124
        def poll(self):
            return self.returncode
        def wait(self, timeout=None):
            return self.returncode if self.returncode is not None else 0

    t = {"now": [0.0]}
    def clock():
        t["now"][0] += 30.0   # mỗi lần gọi +30 phút → vượt deadline 1 phút ngay
        return t["now"][0]
    pops = []
    def fake_popen(cmd, **k):
        p = FakeProc(); pops.append((cmd, k)); return p

    code = agent.run_engine(["x"], cwd=".", log_path=tmp_path / "run.log",
                            deadline_min=1, stdin_text="p", popen=fake_popen,
                            clock=clock, sleep=lambda s: None)
    assert code == 124 and pops[0][1]["stdin"] is not None


def test_run_engine_normal_exit(tmp_path):
    class FakeProc:
        pid = 1
        returncode = 0
        def kill(self): pass
        def poll(self): return 0
        def wait(self, timeout=None): return 0
    code = agent.run_engine(["x"], cwd=".", log_path=tmp_path / "run.log",
                            deadline_min=5, stdin_text="",
                            popen=lambda c, **k: FakeProc(),
                            clock=lambda: 0.0, sleep=lambda s: None)
    assert code == 0 and (tmp_path / "run.log").exists()
```

- [ ] **Step 2 — Run:** FAIL.
- [ ] **Step 3 — implementation:** như Interfaces. `run_engine` loop: `while proc.poll() is None:` nếu `clock() - start > deadline_min*60` → `proc.kill()`, returncode 124; sleep(1) giữa lần poll (fake clock đẩy nhanh test). Log mở append + `errors="replace"`.
- [ ] **Step 4 — Run:** PASS.
- [ ] **Step 5 — Commit:** `feat: P6 T3 agent engine render/preflight/kill-switch`.

```python
# tests/test_runner_agent.py (append)
REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent


def test_agent_assets_exist_and_render():
    for rel in ("agent/opencode.json.tpl", "agent/AGENTS.md",
                "agent/prompts/audit-main.md", "agent/prompts/verifier.md",
                "docs/playbook.md"):
        assert (REPO / rel).exists(), rel
    prompt = agent.render_prompt(REPO / "agent/prompts/audit-main.md",
                                 "com.foo", "com_foo", "C:/a.apk")
    # guardrails + 4-pass phải vào prompt rendered
    for word in ("Pass 1", "Pass 4", "workspace/com_foo", "không tấn công"):
        assert word in prompt, word
    # opencode.json render phải là JSON hợp lệ có mục mcp + permission allow
    oc = json.loads(agent.render_opencode_json(
        REPO / "agent/opencode.json.tpl", REPO, "m"))
    assert "permission" in oc and oc["permission"]["edit"] == "allow"

- [ ] **Step 3 — implementation:** viết 5 file:
  - `agent/opencode.json.tpl` — `{"model": "{{MODEL}}", "permission": {"edit": "allow", "bash": {"*": "allow"}, "webfetch": "allow"}}`.
  - `agent/AGENTS.md` — nội quy runner: phạm vi ủy quyền, chỉ ghi `workspace/{{WORKSPACE_APP}}/`, mọi thao tác ghi run.log, cấm tấn công chủ động remote, stop-conditions (hết turns/paywall→coverage-gap), lệnh mph + MCP tools available.
  - `agent/prompts/audit-main.md` — kick-off: vai trò auditor; workspace `{{WORKSPACE_APP}}`; APK `{{APK}}`; package `{{PACKAGE}}`; trình tự 4 pass (Pass 1 surface map từ manifest.json + code index; Pass 2 static sweep — chạy `python -m mph audit sweep` rồi đọc findings.json, bổ sung bằng code graph; Pass 3 traffic qua Burp MCP :9876 + screen MCP; Pass 4 bounded probes + frida unpin); sau đó **verifier pass**: với MỖI candidate gọi `python -m mph audit verify <app> --id ... --reject --reason ...` hoặc accept; cuối cùng `python -m mph audit report {{WORKSPACE_APP}}`; guardrails đoạn "KHÔNG": không tấn công chủ động hạ tầng remote (scan/bruteforce/DoS/server-side exploit) — ghi vào "Đề xuất kiểm tra thủ công"; không sửa ngoài workspace; tiếng Việt + thuật tiếng Anh.
  - `agent/prompts/verifier.md` — prompt verify 1 finding: chỉ nhận finding + evidence, phải reproduce/bác bỏ bằng repro trên emulator hoặc bằng chứng code, kết luận accept/reject kèm lý do.
  - `docs/playbook.md` — runbook manual mode: 8 bước (surface map, static sweep + verify, traffic setup, lái app, moi /data/data, frida hook, coverage check, report) mỗi bước lệnh mph tương ứng.
- [ ] **Step 4 — Run:** PASS.
- [ ] **Step 5 — Commit:** `feat: P6 T4 prompt/templates/playbook that`.

### Task 5: `mph run` orchestration + `run.ps1`

**Files:**
- Create: `mph/runner/orchestrator.py`
- Modify: `mph/cli.py` (command `run`)
- Create: `run.ps1`
- Test: `tests/test_runner_orch.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `setup_manual` (P4), `RunState` (T2), `agent` (T3), `mph.audit` (P5), `Config` (T1).
- Produces: `run_apk(cfg, apk: Path, mode: str = "ai", serial: str | None = None, on_row=print, deps=...) -> tuple[bool, list[tuple]]`; phase runner:
  - `setup`: `setup_manual(cfg, apk=apk, serial=serial)` — bỏ qua exit chain FAIL chỉ khi device/burp chết hẳn (ok=False → trả hint + dừng, vì không deploy được).
  - `sweep`: gọi nội bộ sweep + save findings.json (như `mph audit sweep`).
  - `ai` (chỉ mode ai): preflight → render prompt/oc.json (ghi `workspace/<app>/agent/`) → `run_engine` → exit code khác 0 vẫn tiếp tục (row FAIL, ghi tiếp).
  - `report`: `audit report` nội bộ → in path REPORT.md.
  - Trước mỗi phase: `st.pending()` — done thì row `(phase, True, "resume: da xong")`; xong phase → `mark_done`. Exit tổng = tất cả row OK.

- [ ] **Step 1 — failing tests:**

```python
# tests/test_runner_orch.py
from pathlib import Path
from mph.runner import orchestrator as orch


class FakeCfg:
    workspace = "ws"
    proxy_port = 8080
    agent_engine = "opencode"
    agent_model = "m"
    agent_max_turns = 5
    agent_deadline_min = 1


def _deps(tmp_path, calls, ai_code=0):
    class D:
        @staticmethod
        def setup_manual(cfg, apk=None, package=None, serial=None, on_row=None):
            calls.append("setup")
            if on_row:
                on_row(("device", True, "ok"))
            return True, [("device", True, "ok")]
        @staticmethod
        def sweep_app(cfg, app):
            calls.append("sweep")
            (tmp_path / "ws" / app).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            (tmp_path / "ws" / app / "findings.json").write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
            return 2
        @staticmethod
        def preflight(cfg):
            return [("engine-binary", True, "ok"), ("api-key", True, "ok")]
        @staticmethod
        def write_agent_files(cfg, app, package, apk):
            calls.append("agent-files")
            return tmp_path / "prompt.md", tmp_path / "oc.json", tmp_path / "run.log"
        @staticmethod
        def build_cmd(cfg, prompt_path):
            return ["opencode", "run"]
        @staticmethod
        def run_engine(cmd, cwd, log_path, deadline_min, stdin_text):
            calls.append("ai")
            return ai_code
        @staticmethod
        def report_app(cfg, app):
            calls.append("report")
            return tmp_path / "ws" / app / "report" / "REPORT.md"
    return D


def test_manual_mode_never_calls_engine(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    calls = []
    apk = tmp_path / "a.apk"; apk.write_bytes(b"PK")
    ok, rows = orch.run_apk(FakeCfg(), apk, mode="manual", deps=_deps(tmp_path, calls))
    assert ok is True
    assert "setup" in calls and "sweep" in calls and "report" in calls
    assert "ai" not in calls and "agent-files" not in calls


def test_ai_fail_still_reports(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    calls = []
    apk = tmp_path / "a.apk"; apk.write_bytes(b"PK")
    ok, rows = orch.run_apk(FakeCfg(), apk, mode="ai",
                            deps=_deps(tmp_path, calls, ai_code=1))
    assert ok is False                      # ai fail → tổng fail
    names = {r[0]: r for r in rows}
    assert names["ai"][1] is False
    assert names["report"][1] is True       # report vẫn chạy
    assert calls.index("report") > calls.index("ai")


def test_resume_skips_done(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    calls = []
    apk = tmp_path / "a.apk"; apk.write_bytes(b"PK")
    orch.run_apk(FakeCfg(), apk, mode="manual", deps=_deps(tmp_path, calls))
    calls.clear()
    ok, rows = orch.run_apk(FakeCfg(), apk, mode="manual",
                            deps=_deps(tmp_path, calls))
    assert ok is True and calls == []       # mọi phase done → không chạy lại
    assert all("resume" in r[2] for r in rows)
```

- [ ] **Step 2 — Run:** FAIL.
- [ ] **Step 3 — implementation:** `run_apk` dựng `app_name` từ diff package khi setup trả về (dùng state trong dict) — cụ thể: sau phase setup, đọc package từ `setup_manual` rows (`apk-package`) hoặc gọi lại `list_packages` diff; đơn giản hoá: deps `setup_manual` trả rows, orch lấy row `apk-package` value làm `app` (nếu không có → `apk.stem`). `state.json` đặt tại `workspace/<app>/state.json`. `write_agent_files` thật (default deps) render T3 + ghi `agent/prompt.md`, `agent/opencode.json`, log `agent/run.log`; chạy engine với stdin=prompt. CLI `mph run <apk> --mode --serial --max-turns --deadline-min` (override cfg). `run.ps1` clone pattern `setup-manual.ps1`: tìm python → `python -m mph run <apk> [--mode manual]` → pause; exit code relay.
- [ ] **Step 4 — Run:** PASS.
- [ ] **Step 5 — Commit:** `feat: P6 T5 mph run orchestration + run.ps1`.

## Self-Review

- **Spec coverage:** §6b preflight(1) = `setup_manual`; deploy(2) = apk-install trong chain + snapshot `pre-audit` — **snapshot bỏ qua v1** (ghi README limitation, rollback = xóa app); kick AI(3) = T3/T4/T5; agent tự làm(4) = prompt 4-pass + verifier; kết thúc(5) = report phase + run.log. Guardrails §6b = AGENTS.md + prompt + test T4. §6c manual = T5 (không engine) + playbook T4. Resume §6b cuối = T2. Risks §11 runaway = deadline/kill T3; login wall = stop-conditions prompt T4. Đủ cho v1.
- **Placeholders:** không.
- **Type consistency:** `run_apk(cfg, apk, mode, serial, on_row, deps)` khớp CLI T5; `write_agent_files(cfg, app, package, apk) -> (prompt, oc_json, log)` khớp T3 render; `run_engine(cmd, cwd, log_path, deadline_min, stdin_text)` khớp.
- **Review Focus → tests:** thiếu engine (T3), treo→kill (T3), resume (T2/T5), braces (T3/T4), manual không AI (T5), ai-fail vẫn report (T5).

```

`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-mobile-pentest-harness-design.md`:

```md
# Design Spec — Mobile Pentest Harness (`mph`)

- **Ngày:** 2026-10-07
- **Trạng thái:** Approved (brainstorming xong, chờ implementation plan)
- **Repo:** `C:\Users\sonnt\Documents\Mobile\mobile-harness`
- **Quyết định chính:** AVD làm device chủ lực · Burp Suite Pro là proxy duy nhất (listener **8080**) · Core lib + CLI + MCP server · Approach "glue-orchestrator trên OSS đã kiểm chứng" · **BrutDroid 2.0 là đường bootstrap chủ lực** (root/CA/frida), harness bổ sung phần nó thiếu · **Điểm vào duy nhất cho người dùng: `run.ps1 <apk>` — tự setup, tự khởi động AI audit, người chỉ đọc REPORT.md**

---

## 1. Mục tiêu & Phạm vi

### Mục tiêu
Xây một **AI harness cho mobile pentesting** cho phép Claude (và con người) chạy trọn pipeline trên 1 file APK bất kỳ:

1. **Phase Recon — "hiểu cách app hoạt động":** môi trường device ẩn danh + traffic decrypt + mã nguồn decompile có index để tra cứu ngữ nghĩa.
2. **Phase Audit — "tìm lỗ hổng bảo mật":** phân tích tĩnh + động theo OWASP MASTG, xuất findings có evidence.

### Mục tiêu vận hành (operating contract)
**Người dùng chạy đúng 1 lệnh:** `.\run.ps1 <path-to-apk>` (hoặc `mph run <apk> --mode ai`). Script tự: setup môi trường (idempotent) → triển khai APK → **khởi động AI agent headless** (mặc định **opencode** chạy model **deepseek-v4.1-flash**; engine hoán đổi qua `mph.toml` — xem §6b) → agent tự lái harness qua MCP (screen/traffic/code) làm **recon → audit (tĩnh) + pentest (động)** → ghi report. **Thao tác duy nhất còn lại của người: mở `workspace/<app>/report/REPORT.md`.** CLI/MCP các lệnh lẻ chỉ dành cho debug và cho agent.

**AI là lớp tùy chọn:** `run.ps1 <apk> --mode manual` dừng sau setup + deploy (không cần claude CLI, không cần API key, không cần MCP) và in "next steps" theo runbook. Toàn bộ CLI `mph …` dùng độc lập bằng tay được; xem §6c.

### Ràng buộc sử dụng (bắt buộc)
Harness chỉ dùng để phân tích **app được ủy quyền**: app tự phát triển, chương trình bug bounty còn scope, CTF/lab, hoặc có hợp đồng kiểm thử. Ghi ràng buộc này vào README và `mph audit`.

### Ngoài phạm vi v1
- iOS / jailbreak (chỉ Android v1; device layer đã abstract nên mở rộng sau được)
- Transparent-proxy mode (iptables REDIRECT) — v2, khi gặp app bỏ qua system proxy
- MobSF / drozer integration — plugin tùy chọn sau P5
- Tự viết lại bypass logic (approach 2 đã bị loại)

---

## 2. Hiện trạng môi trường (survey 2026-10-07)

| Thành phần | Trạng thái |
|---|---|

| jadx | ✅ 1.5.5 (winget) + `Documents\JADX\bin` |
| frida, frida-ps, objection, mitmproxy | ✅ Python 3.12 (`pip install`) |
| Burp Suite **Pro** | ✅ `C:\Program Files\Burp\bin\BurpSuitePro`; có **MCP native** SSE `127.0.0.1:9876` (prior art `Downloads\friendkit_analysis\burp_mcp.py`, `burp_listener_config.json`, `burpui.ps1`) |
| Android SDK | ✅ `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Android\Sdk` (emulator.exe, platform-tools). **Thiếu cmdline-tools** (không có sdkmanager/avdmanager) → bootstrap phải tự tải |
| AVD | ✅ `Pixel_6_Pro` — android-37.1 google_apis x86_64 **16KB-page** (mới quá, giữ làm experimental). Còn android-37.2-beta3 images |

| apktool | ❌ chưa cài (bootstrap thêm nếu cần smali edit) |
| **BrutDroid 2.0** | ⬇️ vendored bởi bootstrap — [Brut-Security/BrutDroid](https://github.com/Brut-Security/BrutDroid), MIT, 642★, maintain active (10-2026), **Windows-optimized**, Python. Làm: root AVD (rootAVD+Magisk, mặc định API 31), Burp CA → system trust (module AlwaysTrustUserCerts), cài/chạy frida-server, script sẵn SSL-BYE/ROOTER, cài objection/reflutter. Thiếu (harness bù): PIF/Shamiko, StrongR-frida hide, Burp-MCP, API 34, snapshot, screen-loop, RE/index/audit |
